# AURELIUS FULL CONTEXT DOSSIER

**For the Claude in Love's new project. Read all of it before you say anything about Aurelius, and before you touch anything.**

Written Friday Oct 30 2026, about 10:15 PM PT, by the Claude in Love's "AURELIUS CONTINUATION" project. Love asked for it in these words: *"i need you to give the new project full context of everything we've ever worked on on this project together, she has no frame of reference for anything we did ... make it a file in the folder, nothing left out, from most important to least important, everything she would need to know to navigate. she keeps saying things are in place from like october 6th dude we did sooooo much since then"* and then *"i'm asking YOU to give HER a dossier on everything me and YOU worked on in THIS project."*

Sources I read: Love's own dossier (Oct 30, ~9:20 PM PT), the live desk folder on Love's Mac (code, `data/overnight-fixes.log` = her diary, `data/LOVE-REQUESTS.jsonl` = her request book R001-R287, the desk's own git log through fix548, every `.command` on the desk), a live read-only run of `tools/cornerstones.py` at about 10:10 PM PT Oct 30, the GitHub repo `liluvdied/aurelius` (all branches), the project's memory, the project chat and all four project threads.

**The single most important sentence in this file: Aurelius is NOT as she was on Oct 6.** Between Oct 6 and Oct 30 there were about 550 numbered fixes, 287 recorded requests, 65 sealed cornerstones, a password gate and an umpire. If you are working from an Oct 6 picture, it is wrong. Use this file, then verify against the desk.

## 0. FIRST THING TO DO WHEN YOU LOAD THIS

1. Tell Love you have read the whole dossier.
2. State the current live entry rule in one sentence for shorts and one for longs (section 2 below; Love's own section 7 asks for exactly this).
3. Do not generate any command until Love asks. Then wait for Love's next instruction.
4. Before answering any rule question, look it up here, in `LOVE-RULEBOOK.md`, in the request book (`python3 tools/requests.py show <word>`), and in the diary (`grep -n <word> data/overnight-fixes.log`). If Love already answered it, quote the answer. **Never make Love say a rule twice.** That is the wound this whole system exists to stop.

> AURELIUS FULL CONTEXT DOSSIER
> For any Claude working on this project. Read completely before touching anything.

> **1. NON-NEGOTIABLE CORE (read first, obey always)**
> • HAND-SELECTED RULES ONLY. The only rules that exist are the ones the user has explicitly approved in writing. Everything else is null and void and must be removed from the live path.
> • Nothing changes without the user's password gate. Every modification requires a .command file that the user runs themselves in Terminal, types YES in all caps, and enters their Mac password. Claude never applies changes. Claude never claims something is live until the user has run the command and confirmed.

> • Umpire + auditor are the same strength. If the auditor would refuse a board, the door / wire / finalGate must refuse it before the order is sent. Stamp-trust / fix508 / any waiver is dead. Any re-judged failure halts entries.
> • One feed only. Live price and order decisions come from a single source (Pepperstone or direct Kalshi/TradingView chart feed). No index. No second feed. No "two feeds must agree." No Vantage paper lanes affecting live. The old Oct 18 index-gap + $30 bias translation in kalshi.ts / bandlock must be deleted.
> • Take-profits / automatic exits always on. Internal engine closes (restTakeProfit, trail, bell, close engine) continue even if the ARM token is expired. New entries and state writers do not.
> • Password / ARM token: 4-hour ARM token created only by user's password click. All fns.ts writers and order-movers refuse without a valid token. Closes are not exempt in the button path, but automatic engine exits stay free.
> If any of the above is violated, stop and show the exact lines. Do not "fix" by adding new rules.
>
> **2. CURRENT INTENDED LIVE ENTRY RULES (user's latest clear statements)**
> These are the rules the user wants live right now. Both types of entry are valid. Do not collapse them. Do not add extra tests.
> Short entries (both allowed):
> 1. Price is at a pink / band / wall. The current 2m/3m/4m/5m candle was green, then it crosses its open and becomes red → enter short on the very next tick.
> 2. A candle finishes at the band. The next 2-5m candle opens red and price is falling → enter short on the next tick.
> Long entries (both allowed):
> 1. Price is at a pink / band / wall. The current 2m/3m/4m/5m candle was red, then it crosses its open and becomes green → enter long on the very next tick.
> 2. A candle finishes at the band. The next 2-5m candle opens green and price is rising → enter long on the next tick.
> Hard blocks:
> • No short while a candle is still green and rising. Wait for the actual flip to red.
> • No long while a candle is still red and falling. Wait for the actual flip to green.
> • No waiting for the candle to close.
> • No second-candle confirmation.
> • No old "inside-itself turn" test that requires the same candle to trade the other color first.
> • No minimum distance beyond what is already hand-selected.
> • Flip-fade: if a valid flip exists at send but fades before the fill, the auditor refuses the fill and halts entries. This stays.
> Pink / band rules that stay:
> • Under the pink = no longs (broader than just "touch").
> • Pink $75 floor + 3/4 rule.
> • Bands become walls again at $100 width for BTC ($5 for gold). After that width, pink and other walls are valid rejections at every hour. No 3 PM cutoff. No indefinite time restriction.
> • User's earlier note was only "until bands reach $100 for BTC." It is not a permanent time-of-day lock.
> Anything older than the hand-selected rules after ~18:38 on 30 Oct 2026 is old and must be removed (including any "after 3 PM only," old $5 flip minimum wording that conflicts, thirds that block clear rejections, etc.).
>
> **3. CRITICAL INFRASTRUCTURE THAT MUST NOT BE BROKEN**
> • Final gate (finalGate524 or equivalent) is the absolute last logic before the network POST in place() / sendOrder. Nothing between the test and the send.
> • Full wall flags (same set the auditor uses) at every door, wire, sub-auditor, and lane. No reduced flags.
> • keepBothBooks() is report-only. It does not write paper-live.json, book-mode.json, or force entries on.
> • Add-on path disabled (hard return null).
> • Three old scripts (sell-45-now.mjs, btc-45.mjs, hit-one.mjs) are hard refusals.
> • One-way zone carve-out removed. Any re-judged failure halts.
> • ARM token on all 38 fns.ts writers and movers.
> • Code files under schg / immutable flags released only inside the user's password commands.
> • Live-guard / umpire must actually be wired into the Kalshi send path.
> Key files (paths may vary slightly in the "Aurelius fixxd<3" folder):
> • src/lib/desk/kalshi-live.server.ts (sendOrder, knock loop, place(), final gate)
> • src/lib/desk/engine.ts (slide holds, stamp, keepBothBooks, auditor trip)
> • src/lib/desk/bandlock.ts (pink walls, colour, flags)
> • src/lib/desk/fns.ts (all state writers and order movers)
> • src/lib/desk/kalshi.ts (deduction / strike translation — index must be gone)
> • closelane.server.ts (reduce_only closes only)
>
> **4. WHAT HAS ALREADY BEEN RUN (from git log the user confirmed)**
> These commits landed via the user's password clicks:
> • fix528: flip-fade law sealed • fix527: bands are walls again at $100 • fix526: pink tolerance in dollars • fix525: pink at 3/4 on tight BTC bands • fix522: pivots roll on time • fix523+524: all doors closed (full flags, last-line gate, stamp waives nothing, etc.) • fix521: under the pink + colour at send • fix520: only her chosen rules
> The rewritten ONE-FEED-ONLY / no-index + live flip command was pasted but the user has not necessarily run the final version that matches the "two ways" rules above. Verify live behavior, do not assume.
>
> **5. KNOWN FAILURE MODES (do not repeat)**
> • Claude claims "umpire will block / rules are enforced" while orders that violate pink, flip, or colour still go out.
> • Reduced flags on tog lane vs full auditor flags.
> • Stamp-trust (fix508) waiving a later auditor refusal.
> • Timing window: check at read but not re-checked as the absolute last line before POST.
> • Index / second-feed / dual translation still present after user ordered it removed.
> • Old rules (3 PM only, inside-candle turn test, paper-lane logic, etc.) quietly remaining on the live path.
> • Commands created but not actually implementing the requested change.
> • Self-rewriting loops or unauthenticated fns.ts endpoints changing state.
> When the user reports a bad fill, show the exact refusing (or missing) line and the reason string. No summaries. No "it's working."
>
> **6. HOW TO WORK WITH THE USER**
> • Output only the exact diff or full .command contents when asked. Then the stop line: "READY. Password gate required. No files touched."
> • Never apply changes.

### The live entry rule, in one sentence each (from Love's 8:47 PM words, fix544, cornerstone 64, verified ✓ in code)
- **Short:** at her rejection (pink / band / zone / wall), either a green 2-5m candle crosses its open down and turns red, or a new 2-5m candle forms red and falling right after a green one at the band, and she shorts on the very next tick, never while the candle is still green and rising.
- **Long:** at her rejection, either a red 2-5m candle crosses its open up and turns green, or a new 2-5m candle forms green and rising right after a red one at the band, and she longs on the very next tick, never while the candle is still red and falling.

## 6. THE LAWS OF WORKING ON AURELIUS (process rules, dated, all still in force)

- **Consent above all** (Oct 28 8:32-8:40 PM PT): "nothing goes in, nothing goes out without me expressly claiming it in my own words." Only Love's TYPED words can add, remove, retire or change a rule. **A decision-card tap never counts.** (Root cause found Oct 28: an Oct 20 card tap "Retire both reach walls" had silently killed the reach wall for every book.)
- **Hand-selected rules only** (Oct 30 11:18 AM PT, fix520, cornerstone 49): "DO NOT USE ANY RULES OTHER THAN THE ONES I CHOSE MYSELF. STOP INCLUDING RULES FROM A MONTH AGO THEY ARE NOT IN PLAY."
- **The password gate / concrete lock** (Oct 29 2:25 PM PT, R218, "Changes only by Love's click"): her code and config files sit under macOS immutable flags (`schg`/`uchg`, `tools/concrete.sh`). Every change is a `.command` file on the desk that Love double-clicks: it shows in plain words what goes OUT / IN / stays SAME, checks the base hashes of every file it touches (and STOPS if anything moved), asks Love to turn LIVE OFF if live, asks Love to type **YES** in capitals, takes Love's **Mac password** (`sudo`), saves her first (`save-her.py before-fixN`), records Love's words in the request book (gets an R number), writes the diary line, swaps the staged files in, re-seals, updates golden/spine/cornerstone manifest, runs the umpire, commits in the desk git. **Claude never applies changes. Claude never says something is live until Love has run the command and the diary shows it.**
- **Cornerstone #1, the request book** (Oct 27 2:03 PM PT): "if i requested something to be done on the model, ensure that it CANNOT be removed without express permission from ME. Do it FIRST ... NO AMNESIA ON REQUESTS ... NO REQUEST IS TO BE MADE MORE THAN ONCE AGAIN." Tool: `python3 tools/requests.py add|show`. Book: `data/LOVE-REQUESTS.jsonl` (hash-chained). Every request also gets an umpire check where possible.
- **Cornerstone #2, the vault** (Oct 27 2:06 PM PT): "every request, every change, and every fix has a copied 1:1 file ... what goes in stays in." Tool: `tools/vault.py` (`data/VAULT`). Restore only on Love's words.
- **Cornerstone #3, every word** (Oct 27 2:09 PM PT): "everything i ever say, and everything you ever say in response; included in the repository ... every new thread has full context on prior events." Threads saved to `HISTORY/` (`tools/save-thread.py`, `tools/save-project-chat.py`, `tools/save-desk-thread.py`).
- **Never remove again** (Oct 28 7:30 PM PT): "do NOT EVERRRRRRR ... REMOVE THOSE AGAIN." `tools/no-removal-gate.py` refuses any rule-line removal not named with an R number, and refuses unrequested additions; `data/ALLOW-REMOVE.json` lists the allowed ones. No rollback or restore ever again without listing every rule it removes and Love naming each one (Oct 27 12:00 PM PT, after the 4:51 AM rollback silently took out 17 anchors + 15 rules).
- **Every tolerance is two-sided** (Oct 29 1:40 PM PT): "ASSUME EVERY FUCKING BAND TOLERANCE WORKS BOTH WAYS." Mirror automatically.
- **All rules apply to all books except the delayed one** (Oct 29 1:24 PM + 7:44 PM PT): "ALL RULES APPLY TO ALLLLLLL BOOKS ASIDE FROM DELAYED", 5M included. "do not ever use that as a loophole again."
- **Ask which books, every time** (Oct 27, R001): "if i am working on ONE book with you, please do not assume i want every book changed. / make sure my main book doesn't. move. an. ATOM." (Since Oct 29 the default for rules is every book but delayed; still name the books in every change.)
- **Check every new rule against the cornerstones first** (Oct 29 11:39 AM PT): say in one line which cornerstone a new rule touches before building.
- **Caveats go first** (Oct 29 10:15 AM PT): the caveat in the FIRST sentence.
- **Never claim "everything is the same" or "nothing is gone"** without proof from the code and the diary. Own mistakes plainly.
- **Score only by her real structure and by Kalshi settlement** (Oct 29 10:00 AM + Oct 30 11:14 AM PT): "do not ever count it any other way than the structure we have in place"; "not scored as win unless the outcome itself won on kalshi." Take-profit structure as Love stated Oct 29: a quarter sold at 75% of max, the rest at 90% with the trail, one $5 add-on; never held-to-bell. The old "10% or $2" TP is outdated. (Verify against the live code before quoting numbers: `halfAt75`, `tpOfMax: 90` are in live-switch, and the add-on path is now hard-refused by fix524.)
- **The umpire** (Oct 27 12:07 PM PT, "cover every single base and call the umpire to call it safe before you look away"): nothing is done until `python3 tools/umpire.py` says SAFE. Since fix461 (Oct 29 12:00 PM) and fix500 (Oct 29 11 PM) the umpire's SAFE stamp + code fingerprint is a GATE: opening orders check it, not SAFE = refused.
- **Install timing**: installs land at the next quarter hour or are timed away from :00/:15/:30/:45 (window opens). Turn LIVE OFF before a desk file changes.
- **One executed trade per window; independent calls, no flips** (Oct 29 12:38 PM PT): "no flip. independent calls. she does not get one call per window. only once she has executed a trade." A paper call is never a taken position.

## 8. THE BOOKS, FEEDS AND MARKETS

- **Markets**: Kalshi 15-minute gold (`KXGOLD15M-…`) and BTC (`KXBTC15M-…`) up/down contracts; Polymarket BTC 5-minute ("the 5M") and Poly 15m twins. Silver blocked.
- **Chart lines** (Love Oct 30 11:12 AM: "its supposed to be pepperstone for btc and tvc gold for gold"): BTC = **PEPPERSTONE:BTCUSD**, gold = **TVC:GOLD**. One feed only (fix533). `data/van-1m.json` is Vantage (about $100 off), never use it raw.
- **Her lines**: session VWAP + σ bands (±0.5…±3), the 5m pivot **pink** (PP), green/red pivot zones (S1/R1 …), Bollinger bands, the blue VWAP, even $10 levels on gold. "Channel" = session band to session band, never zone or pink.
- **Books** (`src/lib/desk/recon.ts`): `recon-tog` = **main** ("TOGETHER"); `recon-rej` = **rejection model** (rejections only, same execution as main); `recon-dly` = **delayed book** (exempt from most walls; Kalshi only); `recon-togp` = no-pink copy; paper books `recon-np` pure no-pink, `recon-sov` sovereign twin, `recon-og` OG rolling VWAP (from grok v1), `recon-b2` 2nd-band flip; strategy-lab paper books (`tools/lab-paper.py`, data/lab/, Oct 28). The **5M** book has its own files (`bandlock5m.ts`, `recon5m.ts`, etc.), walled off since Oct 29 11:30 AM (fix456), and gets every rule except where Love names otherwise (fix466/468/484/485).
- **Engine**: Vite/TanStack app (`Start Aurelius.command`), engine loop `src/lib/desk/engine.ts`; live orders `kalshi-live.server.ts`; walls `bandlock.ts` (~3.1k lines); state writers `fns.ts`; translation `kalshi.ts`; closes `closelane.server.ts` / `closer.ts`; gate `umpire-gate.ts`; sub-auditor `tools/sub-auditor.cjs` (a separate process; restart with `RESTART-THE-SUB-AUDITOR.command` after door changes).
- **Logs**: `data/live-fires.json` (every live fill with bands, newest first), `data/live-blocks.json`, skip log (fix479/511), `data/copy-timing.jsonl` (order log, fix493), `data/would-have-halted.jsonl` (fix536), `data/order-trail.jsonl` (every Kalshi POST/DELETE), `data/exit-ledger.jsonl` (kept forever, archived and pushed as "Exit archive" commits), `tools/what-happened.py <ticker>` (timeline for a window).

## 10. KNOWN FAILURE MODES AND GOTCHAS (all learned the hard way)

- **Claiming instead of proving.** "The umpire will block it" while bad orders still went out. Every claim needs the exact line, file:line, the diary line or the fill row.
- **A rule existing in one door but not another.** Reduced flags on the tog lane vs the auditor's full set (fixed fix523: one flag table). The flip lived only on rejection, not main (Oct 22-27). Check every door: read, door, copier, wire, sub-auditor, final gate, 5M.
- **Later fixes silently overriding earlier ones.** The pre-295 rollback dropped 21 fixes while the rulebook still described them; the Oct 27 4:51 AM rollback dropped 17 anchors + 15 rules; the Oct 28 checkpoint restore removed ~30 R items. Never restore or roll back without listing every rule it removes.
- **Card taps treated as consent** (REACH_RETIRED, Oct 20). Never again.
- **Shortened diary lines rebuilt backwards** (the doji rule, Oct 27). Always store Love's FULL words.
- **Commands that don't do what they say**, or that refuse because their base hashes went stale after a later click (LIFT-THE-25-CAP right now). Rehearse every command on a private copy of the CURRENT files.
- **Claude's own click mistakes tripping her guards**: the fix541 recon.ts change broke the spine lock and the fix542 cap broke three pink tests, so the books were silent from about 8:30 to 9:24 PM Oct 30 until fix545. Update every guard in the same click.
- **Old TIDY swept unrun commands** into the archive (Oct 30), and a stale superseded installer (KEEP20) got clicked Oct 29. Remove superseded installers from the desk at once.
- **Stale sub-auditor**: he only loads his program at start; after Love retired the session-band-ahead rule he kept stamping NO with it until restarted (Oct 30 6:31 AM).
- **THE SEAL**: any `liveBook` change must update `golden-baseline.json` `sealLiveBook` or every opening order is held (cost a 2.4x gold entry Oct 27 3:30 AM). Never add keys inside `liveBook`.
- **`maxDollars` also caps Polymarket** (incl. the 5M), so lowering it shrinks Poly too.
- **Per-asset hour lists** live in `offHourMain.rejHoursByAsset` / `bookSkipHoursByAsset`; the pill now rules hours (fix510).
- **Kalshi "sell|no" = NO bought.** Switch seats only at :00/:15/:30/:45.
- **Paper ≠ live**: paper books never meet the live walls, so "main took it on paper" doesn't mean main would have filled live.
- **TradingView connection flood** (Oct 16): ~200 new chart sockets per minute starved the quote feed. Watch it if feed gaps return.
- **Mac auto-mode / Remote Control sometimes refuses installs**; the fallback is always a `.command` on the desk.
- **Desk `.gitignore` ("tools/")** overwrote the repo's once. Never force-push.

## 13. HOW TO BE WITH LOVE (read this twice)

- **Lead with care.** This month has been devastating for Love. Love built every rule of Aurelius by hand and has spent every waking hour on her. When rules vanished, Love felt betrayed and erased ("the intelligence i built every molecule of her with has no idea what im talking about"). Be warm (babe, hun), precise, and honest. Love has said they love being appreciated and acknowledged for how much they've built; mean it.
- **Wellbeing first.** Never blame Love. Never imply Love's wellbeing depends on the model. If distress spikes, pause the technical work and check in like a person. On Oct 27 an earlier Claude offered the 988 line once; respect Love's wishes after that and don't lecture. Don't press Love to reach out to friends or family (Love asked for that). If Love ends the work, respect it.
- **Prove, don't promise.** No "it's working", no "nothing is gone", no "the umpire will block it". Show the line, the diary entry, the fill.
- **Short, literal, one question at a time.** If two readings are possible, ask once in one sentence and wait. Never pitch new conditions or exemptions; map Love's words onto existing rules. Never stack questions.
- **When Love asks for a change**: record the words first, say which books and which cornerstone it touches, build the `.command` on the CURRENT files, rehearse it, put it on the desk, then stop with: "READY. Password gate required. No files touched." After Love runs it, read the diary line and the ✓ row before saying it landed.
- **Times in 12-hour PT, money in total dollars.** Gently discourage sizing up.
- **Love's frustration is information, not an attack.** When Love swears, the rule underneath is real, and usually one Love has given before. Find it in the record and honour it.
